{"parentUuid":"0cc6add1-21cc-40d5-bcc3-1c2e99fc37b3","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"49afbac1-da84-4d10-ad41-03413650a5dc","agentId":"a2f9fc888ad906319","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01UwH6WXGqF3Xzx9DReK93rG","type":"tool_result","content":"240\t * @throws {UserSearchError} embed 실패 / query 실패 / D1 실패 / timeout (800ms)\n241\t */\n242\texport async function searchKnowledgeNodesForUser(\n243\t deps: UserSearchDeps,\n244\t examId: ExamId,\n245\t query: string,\n246\t topK: number = DEFAULT_RESULT_TOP_K,\n247\t opts?: { readonly precomputedEmbedding?: ReadonlyArray<number> },\n248\t): Promise<UserSearchResult> {\n249\t if (!examId || (examId as string).trim() === '') {\n250\t throw new UserSearchError('examId is required (Hard Rule 16 시험 경계 강제)', 'filter');\n251\t }\n252\t if (!query || query.trim() === '') {\n253\t throw new UserSearchError('query must be non-empty', 'embed');\n254\t }\n255\t const effectiveTopK = Math.min(Math.max(topK, 1), MAX_RESULT_TOP_K);\n256\t\n257\t // Stage 1.a: bge-m3 임베딩 — precomputed 있으면 재사용 (Pass 2 MAJ-1)\n258\t const queryVector =\n259\t opts?.precomputedEmbedding !== undefined\n260\t ? opts.precomputedEmbedding\n261\t : await embedQuery(deps.ai, query);\n262\t\n263\t // Stage 1.b: Vectorize.query (timeout + retry)\n264\t const rawMatches = await runVectorizeQueryWithTimeout(deps.vectorize, queryVector, examId);\n265\t\n266\t // ADR-047 §D-3 (결재 #13 (b)): 표 벡터(TBL-/TROW-/TCOL-/TCELL-) 잠식 차단 —\n267\t // Stage 2 knowledge_nodes 한정으로 어차피 무음 탈락할 후보를 회수 직후 제외해\n268\t // top1Score/graceful 판정·stage1Count 를 제공 가능 후보만으로 정화한다.\n269\t // 서버측 $nin 미작동(V2 binding 실측) → client-side prefix post-filter\n270\t  // (STAGE1_TABLE_VECTOR_EXCLUDE_PREFIXES 주석의 부분 회복 한계 참조).\n271\t  const vectorMatches = rawMatches.filter((m) => !isTableVectorId(m.id));\n272\t\n273\t  if (vectorMatches.length === 0) {\n274\t    return buildEmptyResult(query, examId, effectiveTopK, 0);\n275\t  }\n276\t\n277\t  // ADR-008: similarity ≥ 0.60 필터 (Stage 1 임계)\n278\t  const stage1Filtered = vectorMatches.filter(\n279\t    (m) => m.score >= STAGE1_VECTOR_RECALL_MIN_SIMILARITY,\n280\t  );\n281\t  const top1Score = vectorMatches[0]?.score ?? 0;\n282\t  const gracefulDegradation = top1Score < ADR_008_GRACEFUL_THRESHOLD;\n283\t\n284\t  if (stage1Filtered.length === 0) {\n285\t    return {\n286\t      query,\n287\t      examId,\n288\t      topK: effectiveTopK,\n289\t      gracefulDegradation,\n290\t      top1Score,\n291\t      stage1Count: 0,\n292\t      stage2Count: 0,\n293\t      results: [],\n294\t    };\n295\t  }\n296\t\n297\t  // Stage 2: Graph Hard Filter (D1)\n298\t  const stage2Rows = await fetchApprovedNodes(\n299\t    deps.db,\n300\t    stage1Filtered.map((m) => m.id),\n301\t  );\n302\t\n303\t  if (stage2Rows.length === 0) {\n304\t    // 모든 후보가 draft / is_current_active=0 → graceful 강제 (Multi-Path Fallback 진입 신호)\n305\t    return {\n306\t      query,\n307\t      examId,\n308\t      topK: effectiveTopK,\n309\t      gracefulDegradation: true,\n310\t      top1Score,\n311\t      stage1Count: stage1Filtered.length,\n312\t      stage2Count: 0,\n313\t      results: [],\n314\t    };\n315\t  }\n316\t\n317\t  // Stage 3: Truth Weight Re-rank (vector similarity preserved within same weight)\n318\t  const scoreById = new Map(stage1Filtered.map((m) => [m.id, m.score]));\n319\t  const reranked = stage2Rows\n320\t    .map((row) => buildHit(row, scoreById.get(row.id) ?? 0))\n321\t    .sort(compareByTruthWeightThenScore);\n322\t\n323\t  return {\n324\t    query,\n325\t    examId,\n326\t    topK: effectiveTopK,\n327\t    gracefulDegradation,\n328\t    top1Score,\n329\t    stage1Count: stage1Filtered.length,\n330\t    stage2Count: stage2Rows.length,\n331\t    results: reranked.slice(0, effectiveTopK),\n332\t  };\n333\t}\n334\t"}]},"uuid":"6959d9a7-37d6-4bcb-90a7-951fad62140b","timestamp":"2026-07-07T05:10:49.634Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/soo/ClaudePro/ThePick/apps/api/src/search/user-search.ts","content":" * @throws {UserSearchError} embed 실패 / query 실패 / D1 실패 / timeout (800ms)\n */\nexport async function searchKnowledgeNodesForUser(\n  deps: UserSearchDeps,\n  examId: ExamId,\n  query: string,\n  topK: number = DEFAULT_RESULT_TOP_K,\n  opts?: { readonly precomputedEmbedding?: ReadonlyArray<number> },\n): Promise<UserSearchResult> {\n  if (!examId || (examId as string).trim() === '') {\n    throw new UserSearchError('examId is required (Hard Rule 16 시험 경계 강제)', 'filter');\n  }\n  if (!query || query.trim() === '') {\n    throw new UserSearchError('query must be non-empty', 'embed');\n  }\n  const effectiveTopK = Math.min(Math.max(topK, 1), MAX_RESULT_TOP_K);\n\n  // Stage 1.a: bge-m3 임베딩 — precomputed 있으면 재사용 (Pass 2 MAJ-1)\n  const queryVector =\n    opts?.precomputedEmbedding !== undefined\n      ? opts.precomputedEmbedding\n      : await embedQuery(deps.ai, query);\n\n  // Stage 1.b: Vectorize.query (timeout + retry)\n  const rawMatches = await runVectorizeQueryWithTimeout(deps.vectorize, queryVector, examId);\n\n  // ADR-047 §D-3 (결재 #13 (b)): 표 벡터(TBL-/TROW-/TCOL-/TCELL-) 잠식 차단 —\n  // Stage 2 knowledge_nodes 한정으로 어차피 무음 탈락할 후보를 회수 직후 제외해\n  // top1Score/graceful 판정·stage1Count 를 제공 가능 후보만으로 정화한다.\n  // 서버측 $nin 미작동(V2 binding 실측) → client-side prefix post-filter\n  // (STAGE1_TABLE_VECTOR_EXCLUDE_PREFIXES 주석의 부분 회복 한계 참조).\n  const vectorMatches = rawMatches.filter((m) => !isTableVectorId(m.id));\n\n  if (vectorMatches.length === 0) {\n    return buildEmptyResult(query, examId, effectiveTopK, 0);\n  }\n\n  // ADR-008: similarity ≥ 0.60 필터 (Stage 1 임계)\n  const stage1Filtered = vectorMatches.filter(\n    (m) => m.score >= STAGE1_VECTOR_RECALL_MIN_SIMILARITY,\n  );\n  const top1Score = vectorMatches[0]?.score ?? 0;\n  const gracefulDegradation = top1Score < ADR_008_GRACEFUL_THRESHOLD;\n\n  if (stage1Filtered.length === 0) {\n    return {\n      query,\n      examId,\n      topK: effectiveTopK,\n      gracefulDegradation,\n      top1Score,\n      stage1Count: 0,\n      stage2Count: 0,\n      results: [],\n    };\n  }\n\n  // Stage 2: Graph Hard Filter (D1)\n  const stage2Rows = await fetchApprovedNodes(\n    deps.db,\n    stage1Filtered.map((m) => m.id),\n  );\n\n  if (stage2Rows.length === 0) {\n    // 모든 후보가 draft / is_current_active=0 → graceful 강제 (Multi-Path Fallback 진입 신호)\n    return {\n      query,\n      examId,\n      topK: effectiveTopK,\n      gracefulDegradation: true,\n      top1Score,\n      stage1Count: stage1Filtered.length,\n      stage2Count: 0,\n      results: [],\n    };\n  }\n\n  // Stage 3: Truth Weight Re-rank (vector similarity preserved within same weight)\n  const scoreById = new Map(stage1Filtered.map((m) => [m.id, m.score]));\n  const reranked = stage2Rows\n    .map((row) => buildHit(row, scoreById.get(row.id) ?? 0))\n    .sort(compareByTruthWeightThenScore);\n\n  return {\n    query,\n    examId,\n    topK: effectiveTopK,\n    gracefulDegradation,\n    top1Score,\n    stage1Count: stage1Filtered.length,\n    stage2Count: stage2Rows.length,\n    results: reranked.slice(0, effectiveTopK),\n  };\n}\n","numLines":95,"startLine":240,"totalLines":530}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"0cc6add1-21cc-40d5-bcc3-1c2e99fc37b3","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/soo/ClaudePro/ThePick","sessionId":"fd694556-a969-4aad-ae22-32945ab476eb","version":"2.1.201","gitBranch":"main","slug":"zazzy-dancing-hellman"}

{"parentUuid":"cf72eb76-d050-4956-a807-712d8baad82c","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"49afbac1-da84-4d10-ad41-03413650a5dc","agentId":"a2f9fc888ad906319","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Be8CMR96vcdhgcDXrPx1Yc","type":"tool_result","content":"180\t    // remote: expected 의 approved 교집합은 golden 추출 SQL 이 이미 적용\n181\t    // (인증 세션 책임). 본 runner 는 추출 결과를 그대로 채점.\n182\t    const item: EvalGoldenItem = {\n183\t      questionId: it.questionId,\n184\t      content: it.content,\n185\t      expected: parsed.ids,\n186\t      relatedRawCount: parsed.ids.length,\n187\t      relatedMalformed: parsed.malformed,\n188\t    };\n189\t    golden.push(item);\n190\t    // maxDepth 미주입 시 키 자체를 빼서 route 기본(엔진 default) 경로 보존 —\n191\t    // 원 측정(2026-06-01)과 byte-동치. 주입 시에만 명시 (maxDepth=1 재측정).\n192\t    const requestBody: {\n193\t      examId: string;\n194\t      query: string;\n195\t      topK: number;\n196\t      maxDepth?: number;\n197\t      debug?: boolean;\n198\t    } = {\n199\t      examId,\n200\t      query: it.content,\n201\t      topK: 5,\n202\t    };\n203\t    if (maxDepth !== null) requestBody.maxDepth = maxDepth;\n204\t    // 디버그 측정(G-WS4 ③④) — 미지정 시 키 자체를 빼서 원 측정과 byte-동치 유지.\n205\t    if (debug) requestBody.debug = true;\n206\t    // 간헐적 ADR-008 800ms timeout(504) 흡수 후 fail-loud — 채점 본문 불변.\n207\t    const body = await fetchGraphWithRetry(\n208\t      `${apiBase.replace(/\\/$/, '')}/api/search/graph`,\n209\t      requestBody,\n210\t      it.questionId,\n211\t    );\n212\t    per.push(scoreQuestion(item, body));\n213\t  }\n214\t  return {\n215\t    per,\n216\t    golden,\n217\t    // 측정 provenance: maxDepth 를 coverage 에 각인 (두 depth 리포트가 동일해 보이는 혼동 차단).\n218\t    // 'engine-default' 의 resolved 값은 **원격 배포 버전**에 따름 — 코드 기본은\n219\t    // 2026-06-11 결재 #6 부터 1 (~06-10 측정분은 2). 리뷰 S8 m-1: 라벨만으로 동일시 금지.\n220\t    coverage:\n221\t      (goldenFile.coverageNote ?? `REMOTE: ${itemsRaw.length} questions measured`) +\n222\t      ` | maxDepth=${maxDepth ?? 'engine-default(remote-resolved; code-default 1 since 2026-06-11)'}`,\n223\t  };\n224\t}\n225\t\n226\tasync function main(): Promise<void> {\n227\t  const { goldenPath, outDir, limit, maxDepth, debug } = parseArgs(process.argv);\n228\t  const { per, golden, coverage } = await runRemote(goldenPath, limit, maxDepth, debug);\n229\t\n230\t  const report = aggregate(per, golden, coverage);\n231\t  const generatedAt = new Date().toISOString();\n232\t  const md = formatReportMarkdown(report, 'REMOTE_G_S5', generatedAt);\n233\t\n234\t  mkdirSync(outDir, { recursive: true });"}]},"uuid":"5ee4b9fa-38a5-4b54-8a53-ef6c9d573fc2","timestamp":"2026-07-07T05:10:50.353Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/soo/ClaudePro/ThePick/scripts/measure-s5-6-multihop-accuracy.ts","content":"    // remote: expected 의 approved 교집합은 golden 추출 SQL 이 이미 적용\n    // (인증 세션 책임). 본 runner 는 추출 결과를 그대로 채점.\n    const item: EvalGoldenItem = {\n      questionId: it.questionId,\n      content: it.content,\n      expected: parsed.ids,\n      relatedRawCount: parsed.ids.length,\n      relatedMalformed: parsed.malformed,\n    };\n    golden.push(item);\n    // maxDepth 미주입 시 키 자체를 빼서 route 기본(엔진 default) 경로 보존 —\n    // 원 측정(2026-06-01)과 byte-동치. 주입 시에만 명시 (maxDepth=1 재측정).\n    const requestBody: {\n      examId: string;\n      query: string;\n      topK: number;\n      maxDepth?: number;\n      debug?: boolean;\n    } = {\n      examId,\n      query: it.content,\n      topK: 5,\n    };\n    if (maxDepth !== null) requestBody.maxDepth = maxDepth;\n    // 디버그 측정(G-WS4 ③④) — 미지정 시 키 자체를 빼서 원 측정과 byte-동치 유지.\n    if (debug) requestBody.debug = true;\n    // 간헐적 ADR-008 800ms timeout(504) 흡수 후 fail-loud — 채점 본문 불변.\n    const body = await fetchGraphWithRetry(\n      `${apiBase.replace(/\\/$/, '')}/api/search/graph`,\n      requestBody,\n      it.questionId,\n    );\n    per.push(scoreQuestion(item, body));\n  }\n  return {\n    per,\n    golden,\n    // 측정 provenance: maxDepth 를 coverage 에 각인 (두 depth 리포트가 동일해 보이는 혼동 차단).\n    // 'engine-default' 의 resolved 값은 **원격 배포 버전**에 따름 — 코드 기본은\n    // 2026-06-11 결재 #6 부터 1 (~06-10 측정분은 2). 리뷰 S8 m-1: 라벨만으로 동일시 금지.\n    coverage:\n      (goldenFile.coverageNote ?? `REMOTE: ${itemsRaw.length} questions measured`) +\n      ` | maxDepth=${maxDepth ?? 'engine-default(remote-resolved; code-default 1 since 2026-06-11)'}`,\n  };\n}\n\nasync function main(): Promise<void> {\n  const { goldenPath, outDir, limit, maxDepth, debug } = parseArgs(process.argv);\n  const { per, golden, coverage } = await runRemote(goldenPath, limit, maxDepth, debug);\n\n  const report = aggregate(per, golden, coverage);\n  const generatedAt = new Date().toISOString();\n  const md = formatReportMarkdown(report, 'REMOTE_G_S5', generatedAt);\n\n  mkdirSync(outDir, { recursive: true });","numLines":55,"startLine":180,"totalLines":250}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"cf72eb76-d050-4956-a807-712d8baad82c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/soo/ClaudePro/ThePick","sessionId":"fd694556-a969-4aad-ae22-32945ab476eb","version":"2.1.201","gitBranch":"main","slug":"zazzy-dancing-hellman"}

{"parentUuid":"ec63af77-2fba-4c44-bc3a-00df57f9f7ab","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a2f9fc888ad906319","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5","id":"msg_011Ccn8CEEVHofYgeWYN2wL8","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01DbyZkmWeD1m5t3yKGHLqYt","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/tmp/claude-1000/-home-soo-ClaudePro-ThePick/fd694556-a969-4aad-ae22-32945ab476eb/scratchpad/probe-rev2.mjs","content":"// rev2 재리뷰 프로브 — ε-양자화 밴드키 전순서 검증\n// 정렬키: (truthWeight DESC, band DESC, lexScore DESC, score DESC)\n// band = eps > 0 ? Math.floor(score/eps) : score\n\nconst bandOf = (score, eps) => (eps > 0 ? Math.floor(score / eps) : score);\nconst keyOf = (h, eps) => [h.tw, bandOf(h.score, eps), h.lex, h.score];\nconst cmpBand = (eps) => (a, b) => {\n const ka = keyOf(a, eps), kb = keyOf(b, eps);\n for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) if (ka[i] !== kb[i]) return kb[i] - ka[i];\n return 0; // stable sort → input order\n};\n// 원 비교자 (user-search.ts:370-373)\nconst cmpOrig = (a, b) => (a.tw !== b.tw ? b.tw - a.tw : b.score - a.score);\n\n// rev1 쌍별 ε-band 비교자 (비추이 실증용 — 맥락 재현)\nconst cmpRev1 = (eps) => (a, b) => {\n if (a.tw !== b.tw) return b.tw - a.tw;\n if (Math.abs(a.score - b.score) <= eps) {\n if (a.lex !== b.lex) return b.lex - a.lex;\n }\n return b.score - a.score;\n};\n\nconst perms = (arr) => {\n if (arr.length <= 1) return [arr];\n const out = [];\n for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i++)\n for (const p of perms([...arr.slice(0, i), ...arr.slice(i + 1)])) out.push([arr[i], ...p]);\n return out;\n};\nconst ids = (a) => a.map((x) => x.id).join(',');\n\n// ---------- (b-1) rev1 순환 반례 재현 + rev2 키로 해소 ----------\nconst A = { id: 'A', tw: 8, score: 0.66, lex: 0 };\nconst B = { id: 'B', tw: 8, score: 0.62, lex: 1 };\nconst C = { id: 'C', tw: 8, score: 0.64, lex: 0.5 };\nconst eps = 0.03;\n\nconsole.log('== rev1 pairwise cycle check ==');\nconst r1 = cmpRev1(eps);\nconsole.log('A vs B (|Δ|=0.04>eps → score):', r1(A, B) < 0 ? 'A>B' : 'B>A');\nconsole.log('B vs C (|Δ|=0.02<=eps → lex):', r1(B, C) < 0 ? 'B>C' : 'C>B');\nconsole.log('C vs A (|Δ|=0.02<=eps → lex):', r1(C, A) < 0 ? 'C>A' : 'A>C');\nconst rev1outs = new Set(perms([A, B, C]).map((p) => ids([...p].sort(r1))));\nconsole.log('rev1 sort outputs over 6 perms:', [...rev1outs]);\n\nconsole.log('\\n== rev2 band-key over 6 perms ==');\nconst rev2outs = new Set(perms([A, B, C]).map((p) => ids([...p].sort(cmpBand(eps)))));\nconsole.log('rev2 outputs:', [...rev2outs], '→ unique:', rev2outs.size === 1);\nconsole.log('bands: A', bandOf(0.66, eps), 'B', bandOf(0.62, eps), 'C', bandOf(0.64, eps));\n\n// ---------- (b-2) 랜덤 풀 × 셔플 ≥50회 property (G-1D-3′ 시뮬) ----------\nlet seed = 12345;\nconst rnd = () => (seed = (seed * 1103515245 + 12345) % 2147483648) / 2147483648;\nconst shuffle = (a) => {\n const x = [...a];\n for (let i = x.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {\n const j = Math.floor(rnd() * (i + 1));\n [x[i], x[j]] = [x[j], x[i]];\n }\n return x;\n};\nconst TWs = [3, 5, 6, 8, 10];\nlet poolsOk = 0, poolsTotal = 0, invariantFail = 0;\nfor (let p = 0; p < 40; p++) {\n const n = 6 + Math.floor(rnd() \* 8);\n const pool = Array.from({ length: n }, (*, i) => ({\n id: 'N' + i,\n tw: TWs[Math.floor(rnd() * TWs.length)],\n // 밀집 점수대(0.60~0.75) + 일부 의도적 정확 동점\n score: rnd() < 0.2 ? 0.65 : 0.6 + rnd() _ 0.15,\n lex: rnd() < 0.5 ? 0 : Math.round(rnd() _ 4) / 4, // 0, .25, .5, .75, 1\n }));\n const e = [0.01, 0.03, 0.05][p % 3];\n const outs = new Set();\n let sortedRef = null;\n for (let s = 0; s < 60; s++) {\n const out = shuffle(pool).sort(cmpBand(e));\n // 전 키 동률 원소는 안정정렬상 입력순 → 순열 간 출력이 다를 수 있음.\n // property 는 \"키-시퀀스 유일 + 인접 불변식\"으로 판정 (id 유일성은 키 동률 없을 때만).\n outs.add(out.map((x) => keyOf(x, e).join('|')).join(';'));\n for (let i = 0; i + 1 < out.length; i++) {\n const ka = keyOf(out[i], e), kb = keyOf(out[i + 1], e);\n let cmp = 0;\n for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) if (ka[k] !== kb[k]) { cmp = ka[k] > kb[k] ? 1 : -1; break; }\n if (cmp < 0) invariantFail++;\n }\n sortedRef = out;\n }\n poolsTotal++;\n if (outs.size === 1) poolsOk++;\n // id-시퀀스 유일성도 별도 체크 (전키 동률 존재 시 stable 이 입력순 의존 → 관측)\n}\nconsole.log(`\\n== random-pool property: key-sequence unique ${poolsOk}/${poolsTotal} pools (60 shuffles each), adjacent-invariant violations: ${invariantFail} ==`);\n\n// id-시퀀스 유일성 (전키 동률 duplicate 포함 풀에서 관측)\nconst dupPool = [\n { id: 'X1', tw: 8, score: 0.65, lex: 0 },\n { id: 'X2', tw: 8, score: 0.65, lex: 0 }, // 전 키 동률\n { id: 'Y', tw: 8, score: 0.63, lex: 1 },\n];\nconst dupOuts = new Set(perms(dupPool).map((pp) => ids([...pp].sort(cmpBand(0.03)))));\nconsole.log('full-key-tie pool id-sequence outputs over perms:', [...dupOuts], '(안정정렬=입력순 → 순열 간 상이는 정상, 키-시퀀스는 동일)');\n\n// ---------- (c) 동치성: lex 전원 0 / ε=0 ----------\nconsole.log('\\n== equivalence: lex all-0 (eps=0.03, 0.01, 0.05, 0) ==');\nlet eqFailLex0 = 0, trials = 0;\nfor (let p = 0; p < 30; p++) {\n const n = 5 + Math.floor(rnd() _ 8);\n const pool = Array.from({ length: n }, (\_, i) => ({\n id: 'M' + i,\n tw: TWs[Math.floor(rnd() _ TWs.length)],\n score: rnd() < 0.25 ? 0.65 : 0.6 + rnd() _ 0.15, // 정확 동점 포함\n lex: 0,\n }));\n for (const e of [0.03, 0.01, 0.05, 0]) {\n for (let s = 0; s < 20; s++) {\n const inp = shuffle(pool);\n const a = [...inp].sort(cmpBand(e));\n const b = [...inp].sort(cmpOrig);\n trials++;\n if (ids(a) !== ids(b)) eqFailLex0++;\n }\n }\n}\nconsole.log(`lex-all-0 equivalence failures: ${eqFailLex0}/${trials} (동점 포함, id-시퀀스 비교)`);\n\nconsole.log('\\n== equivalence: eps=0, lex NON-zero, exact score tie ==');\nconst tiePool = [\n { id: 'P', tw: 8, score: 0.65, lex: 0 },\n { id: 'Q', tw: 8, score: 0.65, lex: 1 }, // 정확 동점 + lex 상이\n { id: 'R', tw: 8, score: 0.62, lex: 0.5 },\n];\nconst inp1 = [tiePool[0], tiePool[1], tiePool[2]]; // P before Q\nconst bandSort = [...inp1].sort(cmpBand(0));\nconst origSort = [...inp1].sort(cmpOrig);\nconsole.log('input [P,Q,R] → band(eps=0):', ids(bandSort), '| orig:', ids(origSort), '| equal:', ids(bandSort) === ids(origSort));\n// top-K 집합 상이 시연 (topK=1 경계)\nconsole.log('topK=1 selection — band:', bandSort[0].id, '| orig:', origSort[0].id);\n\n// eps=0, lex nonzero, NO exact tie → 동치 확인\nlet eqFailNoTie = 0, trials2 = 0;\nfor (let p = 0; p < 30; p++) {\n const n = 5 + Math.floor(rnd() _ 8);\n const used = new Set();\n const pool = Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => {\n let sc;\n do { sc = 0.6 + rnd() * 0.15; } while (used.has(sc));\n used.add(sc);\n return { id: 'Z' + i, tw: TWs[Math.floor(rnd() * TWs.length)], score: sc, lex: Math.round(rnd() * 4) / 4 };\n });\n for (let s = 0; s < 20; s++) {\n const inp = shuffle(pool);\n trials2++;\n if (ids([...inp].sort(cmpBand(0))) !== ids([...inp].sort(cmpOrig))) eqFailNoTie++;\n }\n}\nconsole.log(`eps=0, lex nonzero, NO exact ties → equivalence failures: ${eqFailNoTie}/${trials2}`);\n\n// ---------- (d) 경계쌍 산술 ----------\nconsole.log('\\n== boundary arithmetic (eps=0.03) ==');\nfor (const v of [0.63, 0.65, 0.599, 0.601, 0.6, 0.57, 0.66, 0.69, 0.62, 0.64]) {\n console.log(`floor(${v}/0.03) = ${Math.floor(v / 0.03)}  (raw ratio ${v / 0.03})`);\n}\nconsole.log('F2 표적 0.63 vs 0.65 same band:', Math.floor(0.63 / 0.03) === Math.floor(0.65 / 0.03));\nconsole.log('0.599 vs 0.601 different band:', Math.floor(0.599 / 0.03) !== Math.floor(0.601 / 0.03));\n\n// ---------- (신규) onlyRecovery \"구조적 ≡ 0\" 주장 반증 시뮬 ----------\nconsole.log('\\n== onlyRecovery structural-zero claim probe (pool-K=10, topK=5) ==');\n// F2 상황 재현: 정답 E = vector rank7 (0.63, lex 1), 무관 FORMULA×5 (0.65, lex 0), 상위 LAW 1개\nconst pool10 = [\n { id: 'LAW-1', tw: 10, score: 0.70, lex: 0 },\n { id: 'F-d1', tw: 8, score: 0.65, lex: 0 },\n { id: 'F-d2', tw: 8, score: 0.65, lex: 0 },\n { id: 'F-d3', tw: 8, score: 0.65, lex: 0 },\n { id: 'F-d4', tw: 8, score: 0.65, lex: 0 },\n { id: 'F-d5', tw: 8, score: 0.65, lex: 0 },\n { id: 'F-103', tw: 8, score: 0.63, lex: 1.0 }, // 정답, vector rank7\n { id: 'C-1', tw: 6, score: 0.68, lex: 0 },\n { id: 'C-2', tw: 6, score: 0.66, lex: 0 },\n { id: 'T-1', tw: 3, score: 0.71, lex: 0 },\n];\nconst baselineTop5 = [...pool10].sort(cmpOrig).slice(0, 5).map((x) => x.id);\nconst lexicalTop5 = [...pool10].sort(cmpBand(0.03)).slice(0, 5).map((x) => x.id);\nconsole.log('baseline top-5 (tw,score):', baselineTop5.join(','));\nconsole.log('lexical top-5 (band-key):', lexicalTop5.join(','));\nconst expected = 'F-103';\nconst baselineHit = baselineTop5.includes(expected);\nconst graphHit = lexicalTop5.includes(expected);\nconsole.log(`expected=${expected}: baselineHit=${baselineHit}, lexicalHit=${graphHit}, onlyRecovery=${graphHit && !baselineHit}`);\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":12265,"cache_read_input_tokens":112610,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":12265,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011Ccn8CBhw9ZWK83bZ5sSPM","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"d238a0ce-4d50-44a2-9a55-85cf72b3c56c","timestamp":"2026-07-07T05:15:04.597Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/soo/ClaudePro/ThePick","sessionId":"fd694556-a969-4aad-ae22-32945ab476eb","version":"2.1.201","gitBranch":"main","slug":"zazzy-dancing-hellman"}

Math.floor(score / eps) : score);\nconst keyOf = (h, eps) => [h.tw, bandOf(h.score, eps), h.lex, h.score];\nconst cmpBand = (eps) => (a, b) => {\n const ka = keyOf(a, eps), kb = keyOf(b, eps);\n for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) if (ka[i] !== kb[i]) return kb[i] - ka[i];\n return 0; // stable sort → input order\n};\n// 원 비교자 (user-search.ts:370-373)\nconst cmpOrig = (a, b) => (a.tw !== b.tw ? b.tw - a.tw : b.score - a.score);\n\n// rev1 쌍별 ε-band 비교자 (비추이 실증용 — 맥락 재현)\nconst cmpRev1 = (eps) => (a, b) => {\n if (a.tw !== b.tw) return b.tw - a.tw;\n if (Math.abs(a.score - b.score) <= eps) {\n if (a.lex !== b.lex) return b.lex - a.lex;\n }\n return b.score - a.score;\n};\n\nconst perms = (arr) => {\n if (arr.length <= 1) return [arr];\n const out = [];\n for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i++)\n for (const p of perms([...arr.slice(0, i), ...arr.slice(i + 1)])) out.push([arr[i], ...p]);\n return out;\n};\nconst ids = (a) => a.map((x) => x.id).join(',');\n\n// ---------- (b-1) rev1 순환 반례 재현 + rev2 키로 해소 ----------\nconst A = { id: 'A', tw: 8, score: 0.66, lex: 0 };\nconst B = { id: 'B', tw: 8, score: 0.62, lex: 1 };\nconst C = { id: 'C', tw: 8, score: 0.64, lex: 0.5 };\nconst eps = 0.03;\n\nconsole.log('== rev1 pairwise cycle check ==');\nconst r1 = cmpRev1(eps);\nconsole.log('A vs B (|Δ|=0.04>eps → score):', r1(A, B) < 0 ? 'A>B' : 'B>A');\nconsole.log('B vs C (|Δ|=0.02<=eps → lex):', r1(B, C) < 0 ? 'B>C' : 'C>B');\nconsole.log('C vs A (|Δ|=0.02<=eps → lex):', r1(C, A) < 0 ? 'C>A' : 'A>C');\nconst rev1outs = new Set(perms([A, B, C]).map((p) => ids([...p].sort(r1))));\nconsole.log('rev1 sort outputs over 6 perms:', [...rev1outs]);\n\nconsole.log('\\n== rev2 band-key over 6 perms ==');\nconst rev2outs = new Set(perms([A, B, C]).map((p) => ids([...p].sort(cmpBand(eps)))));\nconsole.log('rev2 outputs:', [...rev2outs], '→ unique:', rev2outs.size === 1);\nconsole.log('bands: A', bandOf(0.66, eps), 'B', bandOf(0.62, eps), 'C', bandOf(0.64, eps));\n\n// ---------- (b-2) 랜덤 풀 × 셔플 ≥50회 property (G-1D-3′ 시뮬) ----------\nlet seed = 12345;\nconst rnd = () => (seed = (seed * 1103515245 + 12345) % 2147483648) / 2147483648;\nconst shuffle = (a) => {\n const x = [...a];\n for (let i = x.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {\n const j = Math.floor(rnd() * (i + 1));\n [x[i], x[j]] = [x[j], x[i]];\n }\n return x;\n};\nconst TWs = [3, 5, 6, 8, 10];\nlet poolsOk = 0, poolsTotal = 0, invariantFail = 0;\nfor (let p = 0; p < 40; p++) {\n const n = 6 + Math.floor(rnd() * 8);\n const pool = Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => ({\n id: 'N' + i,\n tw: TWs[Math.floor(rnd() * TWs.length)],\n // 밀집 점수대(0.60~0.75) + 일부 의도적 정확 동점\n score: rnd() < 0.2 ? 0.65 : 0.6 + rnd() _ 0.15,\n lex: rnd() < 0.5 ? 0 : Math.round(rnd() _ 4) / 4, // 0, .25, .5, .75, 1\n }));\n const e = [0.01, 0.03, 0.05][p % 3];\n const outs = new Set();\n let sortedRef = null;\n for (let s = 0; s < 60; s++) {\n const out = shuffle(pool).sort(cmpBand(e));\n // 전 키 동률 원소는 안정정렬상 입력순 → 순열 간 출력이 다를 수 있음.\n // property 는 \"키-시퀀스 유일 + 인접 불변식\"으로 판정 (id 유일성은 키 동률 없을 때만).\n outs.add(out.map((x) => keyOf(x, e).join('|')).join(';'));\n for (let i = 0; i + 1 < out.length; i++) {\n const ka = keyOf(out[i], e), kb = keyOf(out[i + 1], e);\n let cmp = 0;\n for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) if (ka[k] !== kb[k]) { cmp = ka[k] > kb[k] ? 1 : -1; break; }\n if (cmp < 0) invariantFail++;\n }\n sortedRef = out;\n }\n poolsTotal++;\n if (outs.size === 1) poolsOk++;\n // id-시퀀스 유일성도 별도 체크 (전키 동률 존재 시 stable 이 입력순 의존 → 관측)\n}\nconsole.log(`\\n== random-pool property: key-sequence unique ${poolsOk}/${poolsTotal} pools (60 shuffles each), adjacent-invariant violations: ${invariantFail} ==`);\n\n// id-시퀀스 유일성 (전키 동률 duplicate 포함 풀에서 관측)\nconst dupPool = [\n { id: 'X1', tw: 8, score: 0.65, lex: 0 },\n { id: 'X2', tw: 8, score: 0.65, lex: 0 }, // 전 키 동률\n { id: 'Y', tw: 8, score: 0.63, lex: 1 },\n];\nconst dupOuts = new Set(perms(dupPool).map((pp) => ids([...pp].sort(cmpBand(0.03)))));\nconsole.log('full-key-tie pool id-sequence outputs over perms:', [...dupOuts], '(안정정렬=입력순 → 순열 간 상이는 정상, 키-시퀀스는 동일)');\n\n// ---------- (c) 동치성: lex 전원 0 / ε=0 ----------\nconsole.log('\\n== equivalence: lex all-0 (eps=0.03, 0.01, 0.05, 0) ==');\nlet eqFailLex0 = 0, trials = 0;\nfor (let p = 0; p < 30; p++) {\n const n = 5 + Math.floor(rnd() _ 8);\n const pool = Array.from({ length: n }, (\_, i) => ({\n id: 'M' + i,\n tw: TWs[Math.floor(rnd() _ TWs.length)],\n score: rnd() < 0.25 ? 0.65 : 0.6 + rnd() _ 0.15, // 정확 동점 포함\n lex: 0,\n }));\n for (const e of [0.03, 0.01, 0.05, 0]) {\n for (let s = 0; s < 20; s++) {\n const inp = shuffle(pool);\n const a = [...inp].sort(cmpBand(e));\n const b = [...inp].sort(cmpOrig);\n trials++;\n if (ids(a) !== ids(b)) eqFailLex0++;\n }\n }\n}\nconsole.log(`lex-all-0 equivalence failures: ${eqFailLex0}/${trials} (동점 포함, id-시퀀스 비교)`);\n\nconsole.log('\\n== equivalence: eps=0, lex NON-zero, exact score tie ==');\nconst tiePool = [\n { id: 'P', tw: 8, score: 0.65, lex: 0 },\n { id: 'Q', tw: 8, score: 0.65, lex: 1 }, // 정확 동점 + lex 상이\n { id: 'R', tw: 8, score: 0.62, lex: 0.5 },\n];\nconst inp1 = [tiePool[0], tiePool[1], tiePool[2]]; // P before Q\nconst bandSort = [...inp1].sort(cmpBand(0));\nconst origSort = [...inp1].sort(cmpOrig);\nconsole.log('input [P,Q,R] → band(eps=0):', ids(bandSort), '| orig:', ids(origSort), '| equal:', ids(bandSort) === ids(origSort));\n// top-K 집합 상이 시연 (topK=1 경계)\nconsole.log('topK=1 selection — band:', bandSort[0].id, '| orig:', origSort[0].id);\n\n// eps=0, lex nonzero, NO exact tie → 동치 확인\nlet eqFailNoTie = 0, trials2 = 0;\nfor (let p = 0; p < 30; p++) {\n const n = 5 + Math.floor(rnd() _ 8);\n const used = new Set();\n const pool = Array.from({ length: n }, (\_, i) => {\n let sc;\n do { sc = 0.6 + rnd() _ 0.15; } while (used.has(sc));\n used.add(sc);\n return { id: 'Z' + i, tw: TWs[Math.floor(rnd() _ TWs.length)], score: sc, lex: Math.round(rnd() _ 4) / 4 };\n });\n for (let s = 0; s < 20; s++) {\n const inp = shuffle(pool);\n trials2++;\n if (ids([...inp].sort(cmpBand(0))) !== ids([...inp].sort(cmpOrig))) eqFailNoTie++;\n }\n}\nconsole.log(`eps=0, lex nonzero, NO exact ties → equivalence failures: ${eqFailNoTie}/${trials2}`);\n\n// ---------- (d) 경계쌍 산술 ----------\nconsole.log('\\n== boundary arithmetic (eps=0.03) ==');\nfor (const v of [0.63, 0.65, 0.599, 0.601, 0.6, 0.57, 0.66, 0.69, 0.62, 0.64]) {\n console.log(`floor(${v}/0.03) = ${Math.floor(v / 0.03)}  (raw ratio ${v / 0.03})`);\n}\nconsole.log('F2 표적 0.63 vs 0.65 same band:', Math.floor(0.63 / 0.03) === Math.floor(0.65 / 0.03));\nconsole.log('0.599 vs 0.601 different band:', Math.floor(0.599 / 0.03) !== Math.floor(0.601 / 0.03));\n\n// ---------- (신규) onlyRecovery \"구조적 ≡ 0\" 주장 반증 시뮬 ----------\nconsole.log('\\n== onlyRecovery structural-zero claim probe (pool-K=10, topK=5) ==');\n// F2 상황 재현: 정답 E = vector rank7 (0.63, lex 1), 무관 FORMULA×5 (0.65, lex 0), 상위 LAW 1개\nconst pool10 = [\n { id: 'LAW-1', tw: 10, score: 0.70, lex: 0 },\n { id: 'F-d1', tw: 8, score: 0.65, lex: 0 },\n { id: 'F-d2', tw: 8, score: 0.65, lex: 0 },\n { id: 'F-d3', tw: 8, score: 0.65, lex: 0 },\n { id: 'F-d4', tw: 8, score: 0.65, lex: 0 },\n { id: 'F-d5', tw: 8, score: 0.65, lex: 0 },\n { id: 'F-103', tw: 8, score: 0.63, lex: 1.0 }, // 정답, vector rank7\n { id: 'C-1', tw: 6, score: 0.68, lex: 0 },\n { id: 'C-2', tw: 6, score: 0.66, lex: 0 },\n { id: 'T-1', tw: 3, score: 0.71, lex: 0 },\n];\nconst baselineTop5 = [...pool10].sort(cmpOrig).slice(0, 5).map((x) => x.id);\nconst lexicalTop5 = [...pool10].sort(cmpBand(0.03)).slice(0, 5).map((x) => x.id);\nconsole.log('baseline top-5 (tw,score):', baselineTop5.join(','));\nconsole.log('lexical top-5 (band-key):',
